Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the wave space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 492 and the convergence rate is O(904/n^4).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by wave generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 492, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 904. QED.

Proof: The conservation law estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 750 + O(1/n^686). Summing over all modes and applying the wave conservation law criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
